 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Confine piattaforma del manichino: apre e chiude round economici veri.
     2	
     3	PERCHE' E' SCRITTO COSI': imita `backend/app/modules/games/mines/round_gateway.py`
     4	(che e' il modello indicato dalla specifica) e passa dalla stessa giuntura,
     5	`PlatformGameAdapter` in `backend/app/modules/platform/game_modules/adapter.py`.
     6	Il manichino non reinventa il confine: lo chiama con `game_code="manichino"`.
     7	
     8	PERCHE' L'ADAPTER VIVE QUI: la specifica assegna al modulo tre file
     9	(`__init__.py`, `round_gateway.py`, `service.py`); Mines tiene l'adapter in
    10	`platform_client.py` per ragioni storiche, qui resta nel gateway perche' il
    11	modulo non ha altro stato di gioco da nascondere.

    58	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    59	class ManichinoPlatformRoundOpenResult:
    60	    """Fatti di piattaforma prodotti all'apertura di un round manichino."""
    61	
    62	    platform_round_id: str
    63	    wallet_account_id: str
    64	    wallet_balance_after_start: Decimal
    65	    ledger_transaction_id: str
    66	    table_session_id: str
    67	    table_session: dict[str, object]
    68	
    69	
    70	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    71	class ManichinoPlatformRoundWinResult:
    72	    """Fatti di piattaforma prodotti chiudendo un round manichino in vincita."""
    73	
    74	    platform_round_id: str
    75	    wallet_balance_after: Decimal
    76	    ledger_transaction_id: str
    77	    already_exists: bool
    78	
    79	
    80	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    81	class ManichinoPlatformRoundLossResult:
    82	    """Fatti di piattaforma prodotti chiudendo un round manichino in perdita."""
    83	
    84	    platform_round_id: str
    85	    wallet_balance_after: Decimal
    86	    bet_transaction_id: str
    87	
    88	
    89	class InProcessManichinoPlatformAdapter:
    90	    """Adapter del manichino sui servizi di piattaforma in-process.
    91	
    92	    PERCHE' `grid_size=0, mine_count=0`: `open_game_round` e' nato per Mines e
    93	    chiede quei due parametri, ma li usa solo come ripiego nei metadati del
    94	    ledger quando manca `game_config_payload`. Il manichino non ha griglia ne'
    95	    mine: passa sempre un payload esplicito e i due valori non vengono letti.
    96	    """
    97	
    98	    def open_round(self, request: PlatformOpenRoundRequest) -> PlatformOpenRoundResult:
    99	        _ensure_manichino_game_code(request.game_code)
   100	        try:
   101	            result = open_game_round(
   102	                cursor=request.cursor,
   103	                game_code=GAME_CODE,
   104	                user_id=request.player_ref,
   105	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,
   106	                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   107	                grid_size=0,
   108	                mine_count=0,
   109	                bet_amount=request.bet_amount,
   110	                wallet_type=request.wallet_source,
   111	                table_session_id=request.table_session_ref,
   112	                access_session_id=request.access_session_ref,
   113	                title_code=request.title_code,
   114	                site_code=request.site_code,
   115	                request_fingerprint=request.request_fingerprint,
   116	                game_config_payload={"note": "esito deciso dal chiamante"},
   117	            )
   118	        except PlatformRoundValidationError as exc:
   119	            raise ManichinoValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
   120	        except PlatformRoundInsufficientBalanceError as exc:
   121	            raise ManichinoInsufficientBalanceError(str(exc)) from exc
   122	        except TableSessionLimitExceededError as exc:
   123	            raise ManichinoValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
   124	        except (
   125	            TableSessionNotFoundError,
   126	            TableSessionStateConflictError,
   127	            TableSessionValidationError,
   128	        ) as exc:
   129	            raise ManichinoValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
   130	
   131	        return PlatformOpenRoundResult(
   132	            platform_round_ref=str(result["platform_round_id"]),
   133	            wallet_account_ref=str(result["wallet_account_id"]),
   134	            wallet_balance_after_start=Decimal(result["wallet_balance_after_start"]),
   135	            ledger_transaction_ref=str(result["ledger_transaction_id"]),
   136	            table_session_ref=str(result["table_session_id"]),
   137	            table_session=dict(result["table_session"]),
   138	        )
   139	
   140	    def settle_win(self, request: PlatformSettleWinRequest) -> PlatformSettlementResult:
   141	        _ensure_manichino_game_code(request.game_code)
   142	        try:
   143	            result = settle_game_round_win(
   144	                cursor=request.cursor,
   145	                game_code=GAME_CODE,
   146	                user_id=request.player_ref,
   147	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,
   148	                payout_amount=request.payout_amount,
   149	                safe_reveals_count=request.successful_steps,
   150	                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   151	            )
   152	        except PlatformRoundValidationError as exc:
   153	            raise ManichinoValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
   154	        except PlatformRoundIdempotencyConflictError as exc:
   155	            raise ManichinoIdempotencyConflictError(str(exc)) from exc
   156	
   157	        wallet_balance_after = result.get("wallet_balance_after")
   158	        if wallet_balance_after is None:
   159	            # PERCHE': nel cammino idempotente (already_exists) la piattaforma
   160	            # non ricalcola il saldo; lo si rilegge dallo snapshot del round,
   161	            # come fa l'adapter di Mines.
   162	            snapshot = get_game_round_cashout_snapshot(
   163	                cursor=request.cursor,
   164	                user_id=request.player_ref,
   165	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,
   166	            )
   167	            if snapshot is None:
   168	                raise ManichinoValidationError("Round snapshot is not available")
   169	            wallet_balance_after = snapshot["wallet_balance_after"]
   170	
   171	        return PlatformSettlementResult(
   172	            platform_round_ref=str(result.get("platform_round_id", request.game_round_ref)),
   173	            wallet_balance_after=Decimal(wallet_balance_after),
   174	            ledger_transaction_ref=str(result["ledger_transaction_id"]),
   175	            already_exists=bool(result["already_exists"]),
   176	        )
   177	
   178	    def settle_loss(self, request: PlatformSettleLossRequest) -> PlatformSettlementResult:
   179	        _ensure_manichino_game_code(request.game_code)
   180	        try:
   181	            result = settle_game_round_loss(
   182	                cursor=request.cursor,
   183	                game_code=GAME_CODE,
   184	                user_id=request.player_ref,
   185	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,
   186	                safe_reveals_count=request.successful_steps,
   187	            )
   188	        except PlatformRoundValidationError as exc:
   189	            raise ManichinoValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
   190	
   191	        return PlatformSettlementResult(
   192	            platform_round_ref=str(result.get("platform_round_id", request.game_round_ref)),
   193	            wallet_balance_after=Decimal(result["wallet_balance_after"]),
   194	            ledger_transaction_ref=str(result["bet_transaction_id"]),
   195	        )
   196	
   197	
   198	_DEFAULT_PLATFORM_ADAPTER: PlatformGameAdapter = InProcessManichinoPlatformAdapter()
   199	
   200	
   201	def get_default_platform_adapter() -> PlatformGameAdapter:
   202	    return _DEFAULT_PLATFORM_ADAPTER
   203	
   204	
   205	_platform_adapter: PlatformGameAdapter = get_default_platform_adapter()
   206	
   207	
   208	def configure_platform_game_client(client: PlatformGameAdapter) -> None:
   209	    """Sostituisce l'implementazione del confine piattaforma.
   210	
   211	    Come in Mines: l'adapter in-process resta il default, ma un test di
   212	    contratto puo' iniettare un'altra implementazione senza toccare il
   213	    service del manichino.
   214	    """
   215	    global _platform_adapter
   216	    _platform_adapter = client
   217	
   218	
   219	def get_platform_game_client() -> PlatformGameAdapter:
   220	    return _platform_adapter
   221	
   222	
   223	def open_round(
   224	    *,
   225	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   226	    user_id: str,
   227	    game_round_id: str,
   228	    idempotency_key: str,
   229	    bet_amount: Decimal,
   230	    wallet_type: str,
   231	    table_session_id: str | None = None,
   232	    access_session_id: str | None = None,
   233	    title_code: str | None = None,
   234	    site_code: str | None = None,
   235	    request_fingerprint: str | None = None,
   236	) -> ManichinoPlatformRoundOpenResult:
   237	    """Apre il round economico di piattaforma per un round del manichino."""
   238	    result = _platform_adapter.open_round(
   239	        PlatformOpenRoundRequest(
   240	            cursor=cursor,
   241	            game_code=GAME_CODE,
   242	            player_ref=user_id,
   243	            game_round_ref=game_round_id,
   244	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   245	            title_code=title_code,
   246	            site_code=site_code,
   247	            wallet_source=wallet_type,
   248	            bet_amount=bet_amount,
   249	            table_session_ref=table_session_id,
   250	            access_session_ref=access_session_id,
   251	            request_fingerprint=request_fingerprint,
   252	            game_config={"note": "esito deciso dal chiamante"},
   253	        )
   254	    )
   255	    return ManichinoPlatformRoundOpenResult(
   256	        platform_round_id=result.platform_round_ref,
   257	        wallet_account_id=result.wallet_account_ref,
   258	        wallet_balance_after_start=result.wallet_balance_after_start,
   259	        ledger_transaction_id=result.ledger_transaction_ref,
   260	        table_session_id=result.table_session_ref,
   261	        table_session=result.table_session,
   262	    )
   263	
   264	
   265	def settle_round_win(
   266	    *,
   267	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   268	    user_id: str,
   269	    game_round_id: str,
   270	    payout_amount: Decimal,
   271	    idempotency_key: str,
   272	) -> ManichinoPlatformRoundWinResult:
   273	    result = _platform_adapter.settle_win(
   274	        PlatformSettleWinRequest(
   275	            cursor=cursor,
   276	            game_code=GAME_CODE,
   277	            player_ref=user_id,
   278	            game_round_ref=game_round_id,
   279	            payout_amount=payout_amount,
   280	            # PERCHE' zero: il manichino non ha passi di gioco; il campo serve
   281	            # alla piattaforma per i metadati e qui vale sempre zero.
   282	            successful_steps=0,
   283	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   284	        )
   285	    )
   286	    return ManichinoPlatformRoundWinResult(
   287	        platform_round_id=result.platform_round_ref,
   288	        wallet_balance_after=result.wallet_balance_after,
   289	        ledger_transaction_id=result.ledger_transaction_ref,
   290	        already_exists=result.already_exists,
   291	    )
   292	
   293	
   294	def settle_round_loss(
   295	    *,
   296	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   297	    user_id: str,
   298	    game_round_id: str,
   299	) -> ManichinoPlatformRoundLossResult:
   300	    result = _platform_adapter.settle_loss(
   301	        PlatformSettleLossRequest(
   302	            cursor=cursor,
   303	            game_code=GAME_CODE,
   304	            player_ref=user_id,
   305	            game_round_ref=game_round_id,
   306	            successful_steps=0,
   307	        )
   308	    )
   309	    return ManichinoPlatformRoundLossResult(
   310	        platform_round_id=result.platform_round_ref,
   311	        wallet_balance_after=result.wallet_balance_after,
   312	        bet_transaction_id=result.ledger_transaction_ref,
   313	    )

   317	    return namespace_game_round_win_idempotency_key(

   319	        user_id=user_id,
   320	        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   321	    )

   325	    *,
   326	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   327	    idempotency_key: str,
   328	) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   329	    return get_existing_round_win_by_key(

   332	    )

   335	def is_open_round_idempotency_violation(exc: psycopg.errors.UniqueViolation) -> bool:
   336	    return is_game_round_open_idempotency_violation(exc)
   337	
   338	
   339	def is_settlement_idempotency_violation(exc: psycopg.errors.UniqueViolation) -> bool:
   340	    return is_game_round_settlement_idempotency_violation(exc)
   341	
   342	
   343	def get_round_start_snapshot(
   344	    *,
   345	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   346	    platform_round_id: str,
   347	) -> dict[str, object]:
   348	    cursor.execute(
   349	        """
   350	        SELECT
   351	            pr.wallet_balance_after_start,
   352	            pr.start_ledger_transaction_id
   353	        FROM platform_rounds pr
   354	        WHERE pr.id = %s
   355	        """,
   356	        (platform_round_id,),
   357	    )
   358	    row = cursor.fetchone()
   359	    if row is None:
   360	        raise ManichinoValidationError(f"Platform round {platform_round_id} not found")
   361	    return {
   362	        "wallet_balance_after_start": row["wallet_balance_after_start"],
   363	        "ledger_transaction_id": str(row["start_ledger_transaction_id"]),
   364	    }
   365	
   366	
   367	def _ensure_manichino_game_code(game_code: str) -> None:
   368	    if game_code != GAME_CODE:
   369	        raise ManichinoValidationError(
   370	            "Manichino platform adapter received the wrong game code"
   371	        )
     1	"""Rotte del manichino: due endpoint contabili, nessuna rotta anonima.
     2	
     3	PERCHE' ESISTONO: i test della piattaforma chiamano queste rotte per produrre
     4	transazioni vere e verificare contabilita', sessioni e registro. Le regole di
     5	autenticazione sono quelle delle rotte di Mines: bearer obbligatorio,
     6	Idempotency-Key obbligatoria, nessun cammino anonimo.
     7	
     8	PERCHE' LA REGISTRAZIONE E' CONDIZIONALE: questo modulo e' incluso in
     9	`app/api/router.py` solo quando `manichino_attivo()` e' vera; in produzione
    10	queste rotte non esistono proprio (404).
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from decimal import Decimal
    14	
    15	import psycopg
    16	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, status
    17	from pydantic import BaseModel
    18	
    19	from app.api.dependencies import get_current_player
    20	from app.api.responses import error_response
    21	from app.db.connection import db_connection
    22	from app.modules.games.manichino.exceptions import (
    23	    ManichinoGameStateConflictError,
    24	    ManichinoIdempotencyConflictError,
    25	    ManichinoInsufficientBalanceError,
    26	    ManichinoValidationError,
    27	)
    28	from app.modules.games.manichino.round_gateway import (
    29	    is_open_round_idempotency_violation,
    30	)
    31	from app.modules.games.manichino.service import (
    32	    apri_round,
    33	    build_start_request_fingerprint,
    34	    chiudi_round,
    35	    get_open_round_by_idempotency_key,
    36	)
    37	
    38	router = APIRouter(prefix="/games/manichino", tags=["games-manichino"])
    39	
    40	
    41	class StartRoundRequest(BaseModel):
    42	    bet_amount: str
    43	    wallet_type: str
    44	    title_code: str | None = None
    45	    access_session_id: str | None = None
    46	    table_session_id: str | None = None
    47	
    48	
    49	class SettleRoundRequest(BaseModel):
    50	    game_session_id: str
    51	    esito: str
    52	    payout_amount: str | None = None
    53	
    54	
    55	@router.post("/start")
    56	def start_manichino_round(
    57	    payload: StartRoundRequest,
    58	    current_user: dict[str, object] = Depends(get_current_player),
    59	    idempotency_key: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    60	) -> dict[str, object] | object:
    61	    if not idempotency_key:
    62	        return error_response(
    63	            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
    64	            code="VALIDATION_ERROR",
    65	            message="Idempotency-Key header is required",
    66	        )
    67	    user_id = str(current_user["id"])
    68	    try:
    69	        with db_connection() as connection:
    70	            with connection.cursor() as cursor:
    71	                result = apri_round(
    72	                    cursor=cursor,
    73	                    user_id=user_id,
    74	                    bet_amount=payload.bet_amount,
    75	                    wallet_type=payload.wallet_type,
    76	                    idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
    77	                    title_code=payload.title_code,
    78	                    table_session_id=payload.table_session_id,
    79	                    access_session_id=payload.access_session_id,
    80	                )
    81	    except ManichinoValidationError as exc:
    82	        return error_response(
    83	            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
    84	            code="VALIDATION_ERROR",
    85	            message=str(exc),
    86	        )
    87	    except ManichinoInsufficientBalanceError as exc:
    88	        return error_response(
    89	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
    90	            code="INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE",
    91	            message=str(exc),
    92	        )
    93	    except ManichinoIdempotencyConflictError as exc:
    94	        return error_response(
    95	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
    96	            code="IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
    97	            message=str(exc),
    98	        )
    99	    except psycopg.errors.UniqueViolation as exc:
   100	        if not is_open_round_idempotency_violation(exc):
   101	            raise
   102	        # PERCHE' si rilegge invece di fallire: una start ripetuta con la
   103	        # stessa chiave deve restituire il round gia' aperto (idempotenza),
   104	        # non un errore — a patto che il payload sia lo stesso.
   105	        expected_fingerprint = build_start_request_fingerprint(
   106	            user_id=user_id,
   107	            bet_amount=payload.bet_amount,
   108	            wallet_type=payload.wallet_type,
   109	            title_code=payload.title_code,
   110	            table_session_id=payload.table_session_id,
   111	            access_session_id=payload.access_session_id,
   112	        )
   113	        if expected_fingerprint is None:
   114	            return error_response(
   115	                status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
   116	                code="VALIDATION_ERROR",
   117	                message="bet_amount is not valid",
   118	            )
   119	        replay = _replay_existing_round(
   120	            user_id=user_id,
   121	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   122	            request_fingerprint=expected_fingerprint,
   123	        )
   124	        if replay is None:
   125	            return error_response(
   126	                status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   127	                code="IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
   128	                message="Idempotency key already used with a different payload",
   129	            )
   130	        return {
   131	            "success": True,
   132	            "data": replay,
   133	        }
   134	
   135	    return {
   136	        "success": True,
   137	        "data": result,
   138	    }
   139	
   140	
   141	@router.post("/settle")
   142	def settle_manichino_round(
   143	    payload: SettleRoundRequest,
   144	    current_user: dict[str, object] = Depends(get_current_player),
   145	    idempotency_key: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
   146	) -> dict[str, object] | object:
   147	    if not idempotency_key:
   148	        return error_response(
   149	            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
   150	            code="VALIDATION_ERROR",
   151	            message="Idempotency-Key header is required",
   152	        )
   153	    try:
   154	        with db_connection() as connection:
   155	            with connection.cursor() as cursor:
   156	                result = chiudi_round(
   157	                    cursor=cursor,
   158	                    user_id=str(current_user["id"]),
   159	                    game_round_id=payload.game_session_id,
   160	                    esito=payload.esito,
   161	                    payout_amount=payload.payout_amount,
   162	                    idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   163	                )
   164	    except ManichinoValidationError as exc:
   165	        return error_response(
   166	            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
   167	            code="VALIDATION_ERROR",
   168	            message=str(exc),
   169	        )
   170	    except ManichinoIdempotencyConflictError as exc:
   171	        return error_response(
   172	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   173	            code="IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
   174	            message=str(exc),
   175	        )
   176	    except ManichinoGameStateConflictError as exc:
   177	        return error_response(
   178	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   179	            code="GAME_STATE_CONFLICT",
   180	            message=str(exc),
   181	        )
   182	
   183	    return {
   184	        "success": True,
   185	        "data": result,
   186	    }
   187	
   188	
   189	def _replay_existing_round(
   190	    *,
   191	    user_id: str,
   192	    idempotency_key: str,
   193	    request_fingerprint: str,
   194	) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   195	    """Risponde con il round gia' aperto da una start ripetuta.
   196	
   197	    La transazione originale e' andata in rollback insieme alla violazione
   198	    UNIQUE, quindi la rilettura avviene su una connessione nuova — lo stesso
   199	    giro che Mines fa con il repository delle idempotenze. Se il fingerprint
   200	    non combacia, la stessa chiave e' stata riusata con un payload diverso:
   201	    None al chiamante, che risponde 409.
   202	    """
   203	    with db_connection() as connection:
   204	        with connection.cursor() as cursor:
   205	            row = get_open_round_by_idempotency_key(
   206	                cursor=cursor,
   207	                user_id=user_id,
   208	                idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   209	            )
   210	    if row is None or row["request_fingerprint"] != request_fingerprint:
   211	        return None
   212	    return {
   213	        "game_session_id": str(row["id"]),
   214	        "status": "active",
   215	        "bet_amount": f"{Decimal(row['bet_amount']):.6f}",
   216	        "wallet_type": row["wallet_type"],
   217	        "title_code": row["title_code"],
   218	        "site_code": row["site_code"],
   219	        "wallet_balance_after_start": f"{Decimal(row['wallet_balance_after_start']):.6f}",
   220	        "ledger_transaction_id": str(row["start_ledger_transaction_id"]),
   221	        "table_session_id": (
   222	            str(row["table_session_id"]) if row["table_session_id"] else None
   223	        ),
   224	        "already_exists": True,
   225	    }
     1	from decimal import Decimal
     2

    28	def configure_platform_game_client(client: PlatformGameAdapter) -> None:
    29	    """Override the platform boundary implementation.
    30	
    31	    Fase 9a keeps the in-process adapter as the default. Future HTTP/contract
    32	    tests can inject another implementation without changing Mines service
    33	    code.
    34	    """
    35	    global _platform_adapter
    36	    _platform_adapter = client

    43	def open_round(
    44	    *,
    45	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
    46	    user_id: str,
    47	    game_round_id: str,
    48	    idempotency_key: str,
    49	    grid_size: int,
    50	    mine_count: int,
    51	    bet_amount: Decimal,
    52	    wallet_type: str,
    53	    table_session_id: str | None = None,
    54	    access_session_id: str | None = None,
    55	    title_code: str | None = None,
    56	    site_code: str | None = None,
    57	    request_fingerprint: str | None = None,
    58	) -> MinesPlatformRoundOpenResult:
    59	    """Open the platform-owned economic round for a Mines game round.
    60	
    61	    Returns wallet_account_id, wallet_balance_after_start,
    62	    ledger_transaction_id, table_session_id and the serialized table_session.
    63	    """
    64	    result = _platform_adapter.open_round(
    65	        PlatformOpenRoundRequest(
    66	            cursor=cursor,
    67	            game_code="mines",
    68	            player_ref=user_id,
    69	            game_round_ref=game_round_id,
    70	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
    71	            title_code=title_code,
    72	            site_code=site_code,
    73	            wallet_source=wallet_type,
    74	            bet_amount=bet_amount,
    75	            table_session_ref=table_session_id,
    76	            access_session_ref=access_session_id,
    77	            request_fingerprint=request_fingerprint,
    78	            game_config={
    79	                "grid_size": grid_size,
    80	                "mine_count": mine_count,
    81	            },
    82	        )
    83	    )
    84	    return MinesPlatformRoundOpenResult(
    85	        platform_round_id=result.platform_round_ref,
    86	        wallet_account_id=result.wallet_account_ref,
    87	        wallet_balance_after_start=result.wallet_balance_after_start,
    88	        ledger_transaction_id=result.ledger_transaction_ref,
    89	        table_session_id=result.table_session_ref,
    90	        table_session=result.table_session,
    91	    )

   145	    *,
   146	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   147	    user_id: str,
   148	    game_round_id: str,
   149	    payout_amount: Decimal,
   150	    safe_reveals_count: int,
   151	    idempotency_key: str,
   152	) -> MinesPlatformRoundWinResult:
   153	    result = _platform_adapter.settle_win(
   154	        PlatformSettleWinRequest(
   155	            cursor=cursor,
   156	            game_code="mines",
   157	            player_ref=user_id,
   158	            game_round_ref=game_round_id,
   159	            payout_amount=payout_amount,
   160	            successful_steps=safe_reveals_count,
   161	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   162	        )
   163	    )
   164	    return MinesPlatformRoundWinResult(
   165	        platform_round_id=result.platform_round_ref,
   166	        wallet_balance_after=result.wallet_balance_after,
   167	        ledger_transaction_id=result.ledger_transaction_ref,
   168	        already_exists=result.already_exists,
   169	    )

    15	HOUSE_CASH_ACCOUNT_CODE = "HOUSE_CASH"
    16	TITLE_CODE_MINES_CLASSIC = "mines_classic"
    17	SITE_CODE_CASINOKING = "casinoking"
    18	GAME_ROUND_OPEN_IDEMPOTENCY_CONSTRAINTS = frozenset(
    19	    {
    20	        "ledger_transactions_idempotency_key_key",
    21	        "platform_rounds_user_idempotency_key_key",
    22	    }

    46	    return f"{normalized_game_code}:cashout:{user_id}:{idempotency_key}"

    59	def open_game_round(
    60	    *,
    61	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
    62	    game_code: str,
    63	    user_id: str,
    64	    game_session_id: str,
    65	    idempotency_key: str,
    66	    grid_size: int,
    67	    mine_count: int,
    68	    bet_amount: Decimal,
    69	    wallet_type: str,
    70	    table_session_id: str | None = None,
    71	    access_session_id: str | None = None,
    72	    title_code: str | None = None,
    73	    site_code: str | None = None,
    74	    game_config_payload: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    75	    request_fingerprint: str | None = None,
    76	) -> dict[str, object]:
    77	    normalized_game_code = _normalize_game_code(game_code)
    78	    normalized_title_code = title_code or TITLE_CODE_MINES_CLASSIC
    79	    normalized_site_code = site_code or SITE_CODE_CASINOKING
    80	    platform_round_id = game_session_id
    81	    table_session = validate_and_reserve_round_exposure(
    82	        cursor=cursor,
    83	        user_id=user_id,
    84	        table_session_id=table_session_id,
    85	        game_code=normalized_game_code,
    86	        wallet_type=wallet_type,
    87	        bet_amount=bet_amount,
    88	        access_session_id=access_session_id,
    89	        title_code=normalized_title_code,
    90	        site_code=normalized_site_code,
    91	    )
    92	    cursor.execute(
    93	        """
    94	        SELECT
    95	            wa.id,
    96	            wa.wallet_type,
    97	            wa.balance_snapshot,
    98	            la.id AS ledger_account_id
    99	        FROM wallet_accounts wa
   100	        JOIN ledger_accounts la ON la.id = wa.ledger_account_id
   101	        WHERE wa.user_id = %s
   102	          AND wa.wallet_type = %s
   103	          AND wa.status = 'active'
   104	        FOR UPDATE
   105	        """,
   106	        (user_id, wallet_type),
   107	    )
   108	    wallet_row = cursor.fetchone()
   109	    if wallet_row is None:
   110	        raise PlatformRoundValidationError("Selected wallet is not available")
   111	    if wallet_row["balance_snapshot"] < bet_amount:
   112	        raise PlatformRoundInsufficientBalanceError("Not enough available balance")
   113	
   114	    cursor.execute(
   115	        """
   116	        SELECT id
   117	        FROM ledger_accounts
   118	        WHERE account_code = %s
   119	        """,
   120	        (HOUSE_CASH_ACCOUNT_CODE,),
   121	    )
   122	    house_cash_account = cursor.fetchone()
   123	    if house_cash_account is None:
   124	        raise PlatformRoundValidationError("Required system account is missing")
   125	
   126	    transaction_id = str(uuid4())
   127	    wallet_balance_after_start = wallet_row["balance_snapshot"] - bet_amount
   128	    namespaced_idempotency_key = f"{normalized_game_code}:start:{user_id}:{idempotency_key}"
   129	
   130	    cursor.execute(
   131	        """
   132	        INSERT INTO ledger_transactions (
   133	            id,
   134	            user_id,
   135	            transaction_type,
   136	            reference_type,
   137	            reference_id,
   138	            idempotency_key,
   139	            metadata_json
   140	        )
   141	        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb)
   142	        """,
   143	        (
   144	            transaction_id,
   145	            user_id,
   146	            "bet",
   147	            "game_session",
   148	            game_session_id,
   149	            namespaced_idempotency_key,
   150	            json.dumps(
   151	                build_forward_ledger_metadata(
   152	                    game_code=normalized_game_code,
   153	                    title_code=normalized_title_code,
   154	                    site_code=normalized_site_code,
   155	                    wallet_type=wallet_type,
   156	                    platform_round_id=game_session_id,
   157	                    game_round_id=game_session_id,
   158	                    access_session_id=access_session_id,
   159	                    settlement_kind=None,
   160	                    idempotency_key=namespaced_idempotency_key,
   161	                    replay_ref=None,
   162	                    game_config_payload=game_config_payload
   163	                    or {
   164	                        "grid_size": grid_size,
   165	                        "mine_count": mine_count,
   166	                    },
   167	                ),
   168	                separators=(",", ":"),
   169	                sort_keys=True,
   170	            ),
   171	        ),
   172	    )
   173	    cursor.execute(
   174	        """
   175	        INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
   176	            id,
   177	            transaction_id,
   178	            ledger_account_id,
   179	            entry_side,
   180	            amount
   181	        )
   182	        VALUES
   183	            (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s),
   184	            (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
   185	        """,
   186	        (
   187	            str(uuid4()),
   188	            transaction_id,
   189	            wallet_row["ledger_account_id"],
   190	            "debit",
   191	            bet_amount,
   192	            str(uuid4()),
   193	            transaction_id,
   194	            house_cash_account["id"],
   195	            "credit",
   196	            bet_amount,
   197	        ),
   198	    )
   199	    cursor.execute(
   200	        """
   201	        UPDATE wallet_accounts
   202	        SET balance_snapshot = balance_snapshot - %s
   203	        WHERE id = %s
   204	        """,
   205	        (bet_amount, wallet_row["id"]),
   206	    )
   207	    cursor.execute(
   208	        """
   209	        INSERT INTO platform_rounds (
   210	            id,
   211	            user_id,
   212	            game_code,
   213	            title_code,
   214	            site_code,
   215	            access_session_id,
   216	            wallet_account_id,
   217	            wallet_type,
   218	            bet_amount,
   219	            status,
   220	            payout_amount,
   221	            start_ledger_transaction_id,
   222	            wallet_balance_after_start,
   223	            table_session_id,
   224	            idempotency_key,
   225	            request_fingerprint
   226	        )
   227	        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, 'active', %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
   228	        RETURNING id
   229	        """,
   230	        (
   231	            platform_round_id,
   232	            user_id,
   233	            normalized_game_code,
   234	            normalized_title_code,
   235	            normalized_site_code,
   236	            access_session_id,
   237	            wallet_row["id"],
   238	            wallet_type,
   239	            bet_amount,
   240	            Decimal("0.000000"),
   241	            transaction_id,
   242	            wallet_balance_after_start,
   243	            table_session["id"],
   244	            idempotency_key,
   245	            request_fingerprint,
   246	        ),
   247	    )
   248	    platform_round_row = cursor.fetchone()
   249	
   250	    return {
   251	        "platform_round_id": str(platform_round_row["id"]),
   252	        "wallet_account_id": wallet_row["id"],
   253	        "wallet_balance_after_start": wallet_balance_after_start,
   254	        "ledger_transaction_id": transaction_id,
   255	        "table_session_id": table_session["id"],
   256	        "table_session": table_session,
   257	    }
   258	
   259	
   260	def get_existing_round_win_by_key(
   261	    *,
   262	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   263	    idempotency_key: str,
   264	) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   265	    cursor.execute(
   266	        """
   267	        SELECT id, reference_id
   268	        FROM ledger_transactions
   269	        WHERE idempotency_key = %s
   270	          AND transaction_type = 'win'
   271	          AND reference_type = 'game_session'
   272	        """,
   273	        (idempotency_key,),
   274	    )
   275	    return cursor.fetchone()
   276	
   277	
   278	def get_game_round_cashout_snapshot(
   279	    *,
   280	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   281	    user_id: str,
   282	    game_session_id: str,
   283	) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   284	    cursor.execute(
   285	        """
   286	        SELECT
   287	            pr.payout_amount AS payout_current,
   288	            (pr.wallet_balance_after_start + pr.payout_amount) AS wallet_balance_after
   289	        FROM platform_rounds pr
   290	        WHERE pr.id = %s
   291	          AND pr.user_id = %s
   292	        """,
   293	        (game_session_id, user_id),
   294	    )
   295	    return cursor.fetchone()
   296	
   297	
   298	def settle_game_round_loss(
   299	    *,
   300	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   301	    game_code: str,
   302	    user_id: str,
   303	    game_session_id: str,
   304	    safe_reveals_count: int,
   305	    settlement_kind: str = "loss",
   306	    record_settlement_ledger_transaction: bool = False,
   307	) -> dict[str, object]:
   308	    normalized_game_code = _normalize_game_code(game_code)
   309	    cursor.execute(
   310	        """
   311	        SELECT
   312	            wa.id,
   313	            wa.balance_snapshot,
   314	            wa.wallet_type,
   315	            pr.table_session_id,
   316	            pr.bet_amount,
   317	            pr.title_code,
   318	            pr.site_code,
   319	            pr.access_session_id
   320	        FROM platform_rounds pr
   321	        JOIN wallet_accounts wa ON wa.id = pr.wallet_account_id
   322	        WHERE pr.id = %s
   323	          AND pr.user_id = %s
   324	          AND pr.game_code = %s
   325	        FOR UPDATE OF wa, pr
   326	        """,
   327	        (game_session_id, user_id, normalized_game_code),
   328	    )
   329	    wallet_row = cursor.fetchone()
   330	    if wallet_row is None:
   331	        raise PlatformRoundValidationError("Selected wallet is not available")
   332	
   333	    cursor.execute(
   334	        """
   335	        SELECT id
   336	        FROM ledger_transactions
   337	        WHERE user_id = %s
   338	          AND transaction_type = 'bet'
   339	          AND reference_type = 'game_session'
   340	          AND reference_id = %s
   341	        """,
   342	        (user_id, game_session_id),
   343	    )
   344	    bet_row = cursor.fetchone()
   345	    if bet_row is None:
   346	        raise PlatformRoundValidationError("Round bet transaction is not available")
   347	
   348	    cursor.execute(
   349	        """
   350	        SELECT id
   351	        FROM ledger_transactions
   352	        WHERE user_id = %s
   353	          AND transaction_type = 'win'
   354	          AND reference_type = 'game_session'
   355	          AND reference_id = %s
   356	        """,
   357	        (user_id, game_session_id),
   358	    )
   359	    win_row = cursor.fetchone()
   360	    if win_row is not None:
   361	        raise PlatformRoundValidationError("Round is already settled as win")
   362	
   363	    table_session = consume_reserved_loss(
   364	        cursor=cursor,
   365	        table_session_id=(
   366	            str(wallet_row["table_session_id"]) if wallet_row["table_session_id"] else None
   367	        ),
   368	        bet_amount=Decimal(wallet_row["bet_amount"]),
   369	    )
   370	    loss_metadata = build_forward_ledger_metadata(
   371	        game_code=normalized_game_code,
   372	        title_code=str(wallet_row["title_code"]),
   373	        site_code=str(wallet_row["site_code"]),
   374	        wallet_type=str(wallet_row["wallet_type"]),
   375	        platform_round_id=game_session_id,
   376	        game_round_id=game_session_id,
   377	        access_session_id=(
   378	            str(wallet_row["access_session_id"]) if wallet_row["access_session_id"] else None
   379	        ),
   380	        settlement_kind=settlement_kind,
   381	        idempotency_key=None,
   382	        replay_ref={"game_code": normalized_game_code, "round_id": game_session_id},
   383	        progress_payload={"safe_reveals_count": safe_reveals_count},
   384	    )
   385	    cursor.execute(
   386	        """
   387	        UPDATE ledger_transactions
   388	        SET metadata_json = metadata_json || %s::jsonb
   389	        WHERE id = %s
   390	        """,
   391	        (
   392	            json.dumps(loss_metadata, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True),
   393	            bet_row["id"],
   394	        ),
   395	    )
   396	    cursor.execute(
   397	        """
   398	        UPDATE platform_rounds
   399	        SET status = 'lost',
   400	            payout_amount = %s,
   401	            settlement_ledger_transaction_id = CASE
   402	                WHEN %s THEN %s
   403	                ELSE settlement_ledger_transaction_id
   404	            END,
   405	            closed_at = now()
   406	        WHERE id = %s
   407	          AND game_code = %s
   408	        """,
   409	        (
   410	            Decimal("0.000000"),
   411	            record_settlement_ledger_transaction,
   412	            bet_row["id"],
   413	            game_session_id,
   414	            normalized_game_code,
   415	        ),
   416	    )
   417	
   418	    return {
   419	        "platform_round_id": game_session_id,
   420	        "bet_transaction_id": str(bet_row["id"]),
   421	        "wallet_balance_after": wallet_row["balance_snapshot"],
   422	        "safe_reveals_count": safe_reveals_count,
   423	        "table_session": table_session,
   424	    }
   425

   237	    if not is_allowed_game_code(normalized):
   238	        raise PlatformRoundValidationError("Game code is not supported")
   239	    return normalized

     1	"""MAN-05: in produzione il manichino non esiste, qualunque cosa dica l'interruttore.
     2	
     3	PERCHE' IL RELOAD: la registrazione delle rotte avviene all'import di
     4	`app.api.router` (`if manichino_attivo(): include_router(...)`). Per provare
     5	l'interruttore senza rilanciare il processo, il test ricarica quel modulo con
     6	l'ambiente simulato e costruisce un'app nuova; a fine test il modulo viene
     7	ricaricato di nuovo con l'ambiente reale, cosi' gli altri test della sessione
     8	trovano il router nello stato giusto.
     9	"""
    10	
    11	from __future__ import annotations
    12	
    13	from dataclasses import replace
    14	import importlib
    15	
    16	import pytest
    17	from fastapi import FastAPI
    18	from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    19	
    20	import app.api.router as api_router_module
    21	from app.api.errors import register_error_handlers
    22	from app.core import config as config_module
    23	from app.modules.games.manichino import manichino_attivo
    24	
    25	
    26	@pytest.fixture
    27	def _router_ripristinato():
    28	    # Il teardown di questo fixture gira DOPO quello di monkeypatch (ordine
    29	    # inverso di setup: va dichiarato prima in firma), quindi il reload finale
    30	    # vede di nuovo l'ambiente reale.
    31	    yield
    32	    importlib.reload(api_router_module)
    33	
    34	
    35	def _app_con_router_corrente() -> TestClient:
    36	    importlib.reload(api_router_module)
    37	    app = FastAPI()
    38	    register_error_handlers(app)
    39	    app.include_router(api_router_module.api_router, prefix="/api/v1")
    40	    return TestClient(app)
    41

    43	def test_manichino_spento_in_produzione_anche_con_interruttore_attivo(
    44	    _router_ripristinato,
    45	    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    46	) -> None:
    47	    # CK_MANICHINO=1 ma APP_ENV=production: il modulo non si registra e basta.
    48	    monkeypatch.setenv("CK_MANICHINO", "1")
    49	    monkeypatch.setattr(
    50	        config_module,
    51	        "settings",
    52	        replace(config_module.settings, app_env="production"),
    53	    )
    54	    assert manichino_attivo() is False
    55	
    56	    client = _app_con_router_corrente()
    57	    for path in ("/api/v1/games/manichino/start", "/api/v1/games/manichino/settle"):
    58	        response = client.post(path, json={})
    59	        assert response.status_code == 404, f"{path} risponde in produzione"

    62	def test_manichino_spento_senza_interruttore(
    63	    _router_ripristinato,
    64	    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    65	) -> None:
    66	    # Spento di default: fuori produzione ma senza CK_MANICHINO, niente rotte.
    67	    monkeypatch.delenv("CK_MANICHINO", raising=False)
    68	    assert manichino_attivo() is False
    69	
    70	    client = _app_con_router_corrente()
    71	    response = client.post("/api/v1/games/manichino/start", json={})

    74	
    75	def test_manichino_attivo_con_interruttore_fuori_produzione(
    76	    _router_ripristinato,
    77	    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    78	) -> None:
    79	    # Controllo positivo: con l'interruttore attivo la rotta esiste — risponde
    80	    # 401 (manca il bearer) invece di 404. La dipendenza di autenticazione
    81	    # scatta prima del corpo del handler, quindi il 401 e' la prova che la
    82	    # rotta e' registrata e non anonima.
    83	    monkeypatch.setenv("CK_MANICHINO", "1")
    84	    assert manichino_attivo() is True
    85	
    86	    client = _app_con_router_corrente()
    87	    response = client.post(
    88	        "/api/v1/games/manichino/start",
    89	        json={"bet_amount": "1.000000", "wallet_type": "cash"},
    90	    )
    91	    assert response.status_code == 401
     1	# Manichino — SPEC
     2	
     3	Il manichino **non e' un gioco**: nessuna grafica, nessuna regola, nessun
     4	generatore casuale, nessun giocatore lo aprira' mai. E' la cavia contabile
     5	della piattaforma.
     6	
     7	## Perche' esiste
     8	
     9	Decine di test della piattaforma non testano i giochi: usano un gioco per
    10	produrre una transazione e poi verificano contabilita', sessioni, registro e
    11	rotte. Il manichino muove soldi veri attraverso le tubature vere
    12	(`platform_rounds`, `ledger_transactions`, wallet, sessioni tavolo) e l'esito
    13	lo decide chi chiama.
    14	
    15	## Contratto
    16	
    17	- `POST /games/manichino/start` — trattiene `bet_amount` aprendo il round
    18	  economico (giuntura `PlatformGameAdapter.open_round`).
    19	- `POST /games/manichino/settle` — `esito: "vincita"` accredita
    20	  `payout_amount` (`settle_win`); `esito: "perdita"` consuma la puntata
    21	  (`settle_loss`).
    22	- Idempotenza: stessa `Idempotency-Key` = nessuna doppia scrittura contabile.
    23	- Deterministico per costruzione: niente `random`, niente `secrets`. L'id del
    24	  round e' `uuid5` della chiave di idempotenza.
    25	
    26	## Interruttore (MAN-05)
    27	
    28	Attivo solo se `CK_MANICHINO` in (`1`, `true`, `si`) **e** `APP_ENV` non e'
    29	`production`/`prod`. In produzione il modulo non si registra e le rotte non
    30	esistono (404).
    31	
    32	## Sicurezza
    33	
    34	Il manichino non entra nel catalogo pubblico, non compare in lobby e non ha
    35	titolo pubblicabile: il titolo tecnico `manichino_test` esiste solo per
    36	soddisfare la foreign key di `platform_rounds.title_code` e va creato con lo
    37	stesso meccanismo delle fixture di test.

# MAN-03 — i file da ricucire, e il metro per non annacquarli

## Correzione di un numero del contratto

`CONTRATTO_FASE_7.md` dice *136 funzioni in 28 file*. Quel conteggio prendeva ogni file
che **cita** una rotta di gioco. Misurando meglio — solo i file che eseguono davvero
un'azione che muove denaro (`start`, `reveal`, `cashout`, `launch-token`, ...) — il
perimetro vero e' piu' piccolo:

| | |
|---|---|
| File da ricucire | **19** |
| Funzioni di test dentro quei file | **100** |
| Asserzioni da preservare | **905** |
| Chiamate a rotte di gioco da sostituire | **71** |

Cinque file in piu' **citano** una rotta di gioco dentro un'asserzione di instradamento
(`test_admin_contract`, `test_game_reporting_registry`, `test_gmp3_host_neutral_descriptors`,
`test_site_v3_public_renderer_contract`, `test_gmp3_host_neutral_demo_launch`): non muovono
soldi e **non vanno toccati**.

## Il metro

Dopo la ricucitura ogni file deve avere **almeno le stesse asserzioni** di adesso.
Perdere asserzioni significa aver cambiato *cosa* il test verifica, non solo il veicolo.
Un file che ne perde va spiegato per iscritto o rimesso com'era.

    36	    )
    37	    avvio = client.post(
    38	        "/games/mines/start",
    39	        headers={**headers, "Idempotency-Key": "parita-mines-start"},
    40	        json={
    41	            "grid_size": 25,
    42	            "mine_count": 3,
    43	            "bet_amount": PUNTATA,
    44	            "wallet_type": "cash",
    45	            "access_session_id": access_session_id,
    46	        },
    47	    )
    48	    assert avvio.status_code == 200, avvio.text
    49	    session_id = avvio.json()["data"]["game_session_id"]
    50	
    51	    mine = set(db_helpers.get_mine_positions(session_id))
    52	    casella_sicura = next(i for i in range(25) if i not in mine)
    53	    scopri = client.post(
    54	        "/games/mines/reveal",
    55	        headers=headers,
    56	        json={"game_session_id": session_id, "cell_index": casella_sicura},
    57	    )
    58	    assert scopri.status_code == 200, scopri.text
    59	    incasso = client.post(
    60	        "/games/mines/cashout",
    61	        headers={**headers, "Idempotency-Key": "parita-mines-cashout"},
    62	        json={"game_session_id": session_id},
    63	    )
    64	    assert incasso.status_code == 200, incasso.text
    65	    return session_id, Decimal(str(incasso.json()["data"]["payout_amount"]))

    68	def _round_manichino(client, headers, db_helpers, payout: Decimal) -> str:
    69	    avvio = client.post(
    70	        "/games/manichino/start",
    71	        headers={**headers, "Idempotency-Key": "parita-manichino-start"},
    72	        json={"bet_amount": PUNTATA, "wallet_type": "cash"},
    73	    )
    74	    assert avvio.status_code == 200, avvio.text
    75	    session_id = avvio.json()["data"]["game_session_id"]
    76	
    77	    chiusura = client.post(
    78	        "/games/manichino/settle",
    79	        headers={**headers, "Idempotency-Key": "parita-manichino-settle"},
    80	        json={
    81	            "game_session_id": session_id,
    82	            "esito": "vincita",
    83	            "payout_amount": f"{payout:.6f}",
    84	        },
    85	    )
    86	    assert chiusura.status_code == 200, chiusura.text
    87	    return session_id

    99	        _ensure_manichino_game_code(request.game_code)
   100	        try:
   101	            result = open_game_round(

   103	                game_code=GAME_CODE,

   105	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,

   108	                mine_count=0,

   110	                wallet_type=request.wallet_source,

   133	            wallet_account_ref=str(result["wallet_account_id"]),
   134	            wallet_balance_after_start=Decimal(result["wallet_balance_after_start"]),
   135	            ledger_transaction_ref=str(result["ledger_transaction_id"]),
   136	            table_session_ref=str(result["table_session_id"]),
   137	            table_session=dict(result["table_session"]),

   141	        _ensure_manichino_game_code(request.game_code)
   142	        try:
   143	            result = settle_game_round_win(
   144	                cursor=request.cursor,
   145	                game_code=GAME_CODE,
   146	                user_id=request.player_ref,
   147	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,
   148	                payout_amount=request.payout_amount,
   149	                safe_reveals_count=request.successful_steps,
   150	                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   151	            )
   152	        except PlatformRoundValidationError as exc:
   153	            raise ManichinoValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
   154	        except PlatformRoundIdempotencyConflictError as exc:
   155	            raise ManichinoIdempotencyConflictError(str(exc)) from exc
   156	
   157	        wallet_balance_after = result.get("wallet_balance_after")
   158	        if wallet_balance_after is None:
   159	            # PERCHE': nel cammino idempotente (already_exists) la piattaforma
   160	            # non ricalcola il saldo; lo si rilegge dallo snapshot del round,
   161	            # come fa l'adapter di Mines.
   162	            snapshot = get_game_round_cashout_snapshot(
   163	                cursor=request.cursor,
   164	                user_id=request.player_ref,
   165	                game_session_id=request.game_round_ref,
   166	            )
   167	            if snapshot is None:
   168	                raise ManichinoValidationError("Round snapshot is not available")
   169	            wallet_balance_after = snapshot["wallet_balance_after"]
   170	
   171	        return PlatformSettlementResult(
   172	            platform_round_ref=str(result.get("platform_round_id", request.game_round_ref)),
   173	            wallet_balance_after=Decimal(wallet_balance_after),
   174	            ledger_transaction_ref=str(result["ledger_transaction_id"]),
   175	            already_exists=bool(result["already_exists"]),
   176	        )

   479	
   480	def _build_void_idempotency_key(
   481	    *,
   482	    admin_user_id: str,
   483	    target_user_id: str,
   484	    round_id: str,
   485	) -> str:
   486	    digest = sha256(
   487	        f"{admin_user_id}:{target_user_id}:{round_id}".encode("utf-8")
   488	    ).hexdigest()[:32]
   489	    return f"admin:session_void:{digest}"

   492	# PERCHE' dinamico: il force-close admin deve poter raggiungere anche i round
   493	# del manichino quando la cavia e' attiva, altrimenti un round di test
   494	# resterebbe aperto senza modo lecito di chiuderlo. Da spento, il codice del
   495	# manichino resta fuori da questa lista.
   496	_SUPPORTED_GAME_CODES = (
   497	    {"mines", "boxe", "hi_lo", "manichino"}
   498	    if manichino_attivo()
   499	    else {"mines", "boxe", "hi_lo"}

   503	def _normalize_game_code(game_code: str) -> str:
   504	    normalized = game_code.strip().lower()
   505	    if not normalized:
   506	        raise AdminForceCloseValidationError("Game code is required")
   507	    if normalized not in _SUPPORTED_GAME_CODES:
   508	        raise AdminForceCloseValidationError("Game code is not supported")
   509	    return normalized

   511	
   512	def _close_game_round_by_code(
   513	    *,
   514	    cursor: psycopg.Cursor,
   515	    game_code: str,
   516	    platform_round_id: str,
   517	) -> None:
   518	    """Update the game-specific round table to a terminal state.
   519	
   520	    Mirrors the per-game dispatch used in auto-settlement
   521	    (``platform/access_sessions/service.py``).
   522	    """
   523	    if game_code == "mines":
   524	        # SIC-08: materialize mine positions and rng material at close so
   525	        # cancelled rounds remain verifiable like won/lost ones.
   526	        from app.modules.games.mines.repository import recompute_board_for_round
   527	
   528	        mine_positions, rng_material = recompute_board_for_round(
   529	            cursor,
   530	            session_id=platform_round_id,
   531	        )
   532	        cursor.execute(
   533	            """
   534	            UPDATE mines_game_rounds
   535	            SET
   536	                status = 'cancelled',
   537	                mine_positions_json = %s::jsonb,
   538	                rng_material = %s,
   539	                closed_at = now()
   540	            WHERE platform_round_id = %s
   541	            """,
   542	            (json.dumps(mine_positions), rng_material, platform_round_id),
   543	        )
   544	    elif game_code == "boxe":
   545	        cursor.execute(
   546	            """
   547	            UPDATE boxe_rounds
   548	            SET
   549	                status = 'cancelled',
   550	                outcome = 'admin_force_close',
   551	                terminal_reason = 'admin_force_close',
   552	                final_payout_amount = 0,
   553	                closed_at = now(),
   554	                updated_at = now()
   555	            WHERE platform_round_id = %s
   556	            """,
   557	            (platform_round_id,),
   558	        )
   559	    elif game_code == "hi_lo":
   560	        cursor.execute(
   561	            """
   562	            UPDATE hi_lo_rounds
   563	            SET
   564	                status = 'cancelled',
   565	                outcome = 'admin_force_close',
   566	                terminal_reason = 'admin_force_close',
   567	                final_payout_amount = 0,
   568	                closed_at = now(),
   569	                updated_at = now()
   570	            WHERE platform_round_id = %s
   571	            """,
   572	            (platform_round_id,),
   573	        )
   574	    elif game_code == "manichino":
   575	        # PERCHE' nessuna UPDATE: il manichino non ha una tabella dei round
   576	        # propria — e' una cavia contabile, non un gioco. Lo stato terminale
   577	        # lo scrive `force_cancel_platform_round` su platform_rounds.
   578	        pass
   579

   581	def _normalize_reason(reason: str) -> str:
   582	    normalized = reason.strip()
   583	    if not normalized:
   584	        raise AdminForceCloseValidationError("Reason is required")
   585	    if len(normalized) > 255:
   586	        raise AdminForceCloseValidationError("Reason is too long")
   587	    return normalized
